The digits are 1, 9, 7, 5, 4, 2. therefore the sum is 1 + 9 + 7 + 5 + 4 + 2 = 14. 14 is not a multiple 3. therefore we must add a digit to the units digit to make the sum a multiple. The only digit that can be added to the unit digit to get a sum a multiples of 3 is 4. therefore 197446
```
